## PromptMaker 6.0 — local image and scene planning

Images & design · Storyboarding, narrative & comics · Video, animation & film

The repository was created on 2026-10-25. This is its first full guide in this library, not independent validation of a new release.

### Quality assessment: Quality unverified

Software licensing and a concrete creative AI use are supported. The remaining checks do not establish a recommendation.

Scope: Develop a character or storyboard, compare prompts against one seed, and build a short music-backed scene sequence while retaining each scene’s settings. The scoped video workflow requires separately installed generation backends.

Evidence checked: 2026-11-09. No local execution implied.

- **Open-source software license · verified:** The complete root software license was read and archived. The grant covers this project code; unresolved model, service and asset terms are disclosed separately. [Source](https://github.com/Kisahime/promptmaker/blob/main/LICENSE)
- **Creative results · documented:** The README supplies UI screenshots and the official v6.0 release describes an owner-tested FastWan 5B workflow on an 8 GB GPU. Gallery, A/B, auto-refine, LoRA and image-to-prompt screenshot images are explicitly abstract placeholders; all screenshots use demo data. They are not independent output-quality evidence. [Source](https://github.com/Kisahime/promptmaker/blob/main/README.md) [Source](https://github.com/Kisahime/promptmaker/releases/tag/v6.0.0)
- **Reproducible setup · documented:** Python 3.10+ is documented. On Windows, inspect and use the repository’s install.bat, then run.bat. macOS/Linux use install.sh and run.sh and are explicitly less tested. Configure a local text/vision provider and an existing Forge/ComfyUI installation; add FFmpeg and Wan2GP only for the selected video route. Setup can download models, while the v6 animation preflight itself is offline. Independent reproduction has not been established in this review. [Source](https://github.com/Kisahime/promptmaker/blob/main/README.md) [Source](https://github.com/Kisahime/promptmaker/releases/tag/v6.0.0)
- **Maintenance and support · unknown:** A release or active repository alone does not establish sustained support. Workflow-specific support and unresolved blockers need a deeper review.
- **Independent use · unknown:** Independent first-hand use or a working third-party integration was not established for this selected workflow.
- **Models, services and costs · documented:** The complete MIT project grant was reviewed. No image model is bundled. Text/vision models, image/video checkpoints, face/segmentation models, creative LoRAs, optional trainers, backends and user music/media keep their own terms. Civitai metadata, folder names and file hashes do not establish compatible or commercially usable model rights. Local compute and separately obtained models/backends. Optional cloud providers may charge and receive prompts/images; a private gist sync is optional. This guide neither configures a provider nor creates a token. [Source](https://github.com/Kisahime/promptmaker/blob/main/README.md) [Source](https://github.com/Kisahime/promptmaker/releases/tag/v6.0.0)

Documentation review only; AI Media Scout did not install or run this software. An 8 GB GPU is an owner-tested FastWan 5B example, not a universal minimum or a guarantee for other presets. Start/end-frame, continuation and camera-control animation workflows are not included in v6. A successful file preflight does not prove a render will run or look good. Optional cloud providers receive prompts/images; keys use the OS keyring or a documented plaintext fallback. No independent reproduction, support or output-quality pass was established.

### How it uses AI

A local text/vision model refines image prompts and plans scenes, while connected Forge/ComfyUI backends generate stills and Wan2GP optionally animates them. The November 9 v6.0 release adds model presets, creative LoRA selection and recorded per-scene animation settings. [Source](https://github.com/Kisahime/promptmaker/blob/main/README.md) [Source](https://github.com/Kisahime/promptmaker/releases/tag/v6.0.0)

### Introduction

A local text/vision model refines image prompts and plans scenes, while connected Forge/ComfyUI backends generate stills and Wan2GP optionally animates them. The November 9 v6.0 release adds model presets, creative LoRA selection and recorded per-scene animation settings. [Source 1](https://github.com/Kisahime/promptmaker/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/Kisahime/promptmaker/releases/tag/v6.0.0)

### What it is good for

Develop a character or storyboard, compare prompts against one seed, and build a short music-backed scene sequence while retaining each scene’s settings. The scoped video workflow requires separately installed generation backends. [Source 1](https://github.com/Kisahime/promptmaker/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/Kisahime/promptmaker/releases/tag/v6.0.0)

### Demo & examples

The README supplies UI screenshots and the official v6.0 release describes an owner-tested FastWan 5B workflow on an 8 GB GPU. Gallery, A/B, auto-refine, LoRA and image-to-prompt screenshot images are explicitly abstract placeholders; all screenshots use demo data. They are not independent output-quality evidence. [Source 1](https://github.com/Kisahime/promptmaker/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/Kisahime/promptmaker/releases/tag/v6.0.0)

### Install

Python 3.10+ is documented. On Windows, inspect and use the repository’s install.bat, then run.bat. macOS/Linux use install.sh and run.sh and are explicitly less tested. Configure a local text/vision provider and an existing Forge/ComfyUI installation; add FFmpeg and Wan2GP only for the selected video route. Setup can download models, while the v6 animation preflight itself is offline. [Source 1](https://github.com/Kisahime/promptmaker/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/Kisahime/promptmaker/releases/tag/v6.0.0)

1. Obtain the official source or release linked below.
2. Python 3.10+ is documented. On Windows, inspect and use the repository’s install.bat, then run.bat. macOS/Linux use install.sh and run.sh and are explicitly less tested. Configure a local text/vision provider and an existing Forge/ComfyUI installation; add FFmpeg and Wan2GP only for the selected video route. Setup can download models, while the v6 animation preflight itself is offline.

```sh
git clone https://github.com/Kisahime/promptmaker.git
```


```sh
cd promptmaker
```


```sh
install.bat
```


```sh
run.bat
```

### First project

Start with a short, representative project and inspect the result before a larger batch. [Source 1](https://github.com/Kisahime/promptmaker/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/Kisahime/promptmaker/releases/tag/v6.0.0)

1. Use the displayed commands on Windows; macOS/Linux use the separately documented ./install.sh and ./run.sh routes.
2. Choose Settings → AI provider, test the actual local text/vision models and connect an existing image backend with a licensed checkpoint.
3. For Video maker, plan a few scenes, edit prompts, lengths and captions, then make and inspect stills before animating selected scenes.
4. Select the installed Wan2GP preset and optional creative LoRA; inspect missing-file and compatibility warnings and start with a short scene.
5. Build the MP4 with FFmpeg and review every frame, caption and music right. Preserve scene settings and project files before upgrading.
### Hardware & software

Requirements below are documented for this workflow. Unspecified limits are not inferred. [Source 1](https://github.com/Kisahime/promptmaker/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/Kisahime/promptmaker/releases/tag/v6.0.0)

- **Hardware:** No universal RAM, VRAM or total-disk minimum is documented. The owner reports FastWan 5B at 1280×704/121 frames on an 8 GB GPU; other presets can need more. Optional LoRA training separately needs NVIDIA: an 8 GB preset is documented, 16 GB+ recommended, with about 6 GB trainer setup before datasets/checkpoints.
- **Software:** Python 3.10+, PySide6 and keyring; face masks add OpenCV/onnxruntime. Local Ollama or an OpenAI-compatible text/vision server is optional; actual image generation needs Forge/A1111 with its API or ComfyUI. Video assembly needs FFmpeg; animation needs a compatible Wan2GP version with --offline support and downloaded models. Linux Qt runtime libraries are listed in the README.
- **Platforms:** Windows is the main documented install. macOS/Linux should work according to the developer but are less tested; that does not verify every GPU backend on those systems. A headless UI test is not cross-platform model execution.

Text/vision models, image/video checkpoints, face/segmentation models, creative LoRAs, optional trainers, backends and user music/media keep their own terms. Civitai metadata, folder names and file hashes do not establish compatible or commercially usable model rights.
- **Cost:** Local compute and separately obtained models/backends. Optional cloud providers may charge and receive prompts/images; a private gist sync is optional. This guide neither configures a provider nor creates a token.

### Why it merits attention

Included for the documented creative workflow. A complete guide and popularity do not establish output reliability. [Source 1](https://github.com/Kisahime/promptmaker/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/Kisahime/promptmaker/releases/tag/v6.0.0)

### Limitations

An 8 GB GPU is an owner-tested FastWan 5B example, not a universal minimum or a guarantee for other presets. Start/end-frame, continuation and camera-control animation workflows are not included in v6. A successful file preflight does not prove a render will run or look good. Optional cloud providers receive prompts/images; keys use the OS keyring or a documented plaintext fallback. No independent reproduction, support or output-quality pass was established. [Source 1](https://github.com/Kisahime/promptmaker/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/Kisahime/promptmaker/releases/tag/v6.0.0)

### Get the tool

- [Repository](https://github.com/Kisahime/promptmaker)
- [Documentation](https://github.com/Kisahime/promptmaker/blob/main/README.md)
